# Changelog

All notable changes to this project are documented here. The format follows
[Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/) and versions follow
[Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/).

## [0.18.4] - 2026-11-21

### Added
- web: move memories management from sidebar into plugins page (27ff8fc)
- memory: enable proactive memory tools, keyword injection, and CRUD by id (69f7e6d)
- workspace: run workspace turns on native Pi (0389fc8)
- coding: add native Pi workspace runner (7574c72)
